docs/06_powerbi_report.md:77: ## Presentation note
docs/06_powerbi_report.md:78: 
docs/06_powerbi_report.md:79: The dashboard screenshots originally used stronger weather and data-centre claims. Update the titles **in Power BI Desktop** before exporting a refreshed report. The changes in this repository do not modify the local `.pbix` file. In particular, describe the NOAA regression as association rather than cause, call the EIA delta 'major-event-day exclusions', and avoid attributing all commercial sales growth to data centres.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:1: # 07. Governance and runbook
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:2: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:3: > **At a glance:** Quick read: this is a reproduction and security reference, not evidence that every optional deployment or scheduled task is running.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:4: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:5: Access control, secrets handling, layering rules, refresh procedure and change control.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:6: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:7: ## Access model
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:8: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:9: | Principal | Role | Permitted | Not permitted |
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:10: |---|---|---|---|
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:11: | Build user | `ACCOUNTADMIN` on the project account | Create and replace all objects in `RELIABILITY` | |
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:12: | `POWERBI_SVC` | `BI_READER` | `SELECT` on all tables and views in `RELIABILITY.MART`, including objects created later (`FUTURE` grants). `USAGE` on the warehouse | Read `RAW` or `STG`. Write to any object. Access any other database |
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:13: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:14: The report connects as `POWERBI_SVC` and can read only the mart. `sql/08_grants.sql` creates the role and user. Two points of note:
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:15: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:16: - `CREATE OR REPLACE` on a table or view drops its grants. Two measures are in place: `GRANT SELECT ON FUTURE TABLES/VIEWS` so that new and rebuilt objects are readable and `COPY GRANTS` on every `CREATE OR REPLACE` in MART so that existing objects retain their grants through a rebuild.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:17: - Power BI's Snowflake connector with username and password authentication requires the user to be `TYPE = LEGACY_SERVICE`. The default user type fails authentication.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:18: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:19: ## Secrets
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:20: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:21: No password, key or token appears in the repository. `08_grants.sql` contains a placeholder. The service password is held in the Snowflake account and in the Power BI credential store on the build machine. In a shared environment the service user would use key-pair authentication with the private key in a secrets manager and scheduled rotation.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:22: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:23: `.gitignore` excludes `*.pbix` (binary; embeds the connection), `*.env`, `*.key`, `*.p8` and the large raw downloads.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:24: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:25: ## Layering
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:26: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:27: | Layer | Reads from | Read by | Rebuild |
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:28: |---|---|---|---|
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:29: | `RAW` | stages | `STG` | Re-upload and `COPY INTO` |
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:30: | `STG` | `RAW` | `MART` | Immediate (views) |
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:31: | `MART` | `STG`, other `MART` objects | Power BI, analysts | Seconds |
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:32: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:33: A change to a source file affects `RAW` only. A change to a definition affects one view in `STG` or one object in `MART` and consumers receive it on refresh. The tests verify that a change has not broken the layer contract.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:34: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:35: ## Refresh procedure
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:36: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:37: ### Annual, October (EIA-861 final release)
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:38: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:39: 1. Download the new `Reliability_YYYY.xlsx` and `Service_Territory_YYYY.xlsx`; save the named sheets as CSV into `data/input/` and `data/reference/`.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:40: 2. Run `scripts/extract_pudl.py` once PUDL has published the prior year's tables.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:41: 3. Upload to the stages. Run `sql/01`. The `RELIABILITY_2025` view and its `source` string are renamed for the new year.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:42: 4. Run `sql/02` and `sql/04`. All tests except T3 and T5 must return zero rows; T3's count must be explained.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:43: 5. Update the year windows in `powerbi/measures.dax` (`IN {...}` sets and the three-year measures).
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:44: 6. Refresh Power BI. Compare the scorecard with the new annual report. Record the load in `CHANGELOG.md`.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:45: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:46: ### Monthly (NOAA)
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:47: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:48: NOAA re-issues files with a new `_c` stamp when events are revised. Run `scripts/download_noaa.sh`, then `sql/05`. T8 indicates whether the latest year is complete.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:49: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:50: ### As required (EIA-930)
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:51: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:52: Run `scripts/download_eia930.sh`, then `sql/06` and `sql/07`. The file for the current half-year grows daily.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:53: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:54: ### Example only: scheduling the tests in Snowflake
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:55: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:56: ```sql
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:57: CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE RELIABILITY.MART.TEST_RESULTS (run_at TIMESTAMP_NTZ, test VARCHAR, rows_returned NUMBER, expected VARCHAR, status VARCHAR);
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:58: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:59: CREATE OR REPLACE TASK RELIABILITY.MART.RUN_TESTS
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:60:   WAREHOUSE = SNOWFLAKE_LEARNING_WH
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:61:   SCHEDULE = 'USING CRON 0 6 15 10 * America/New_York'   -- 15 October, after the EIA release
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:62: AS
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:63: INSERT INTO RELIABILITY.MART.TEST_RESULTS
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:64: SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'T1', COUNT(*), '0', IFF(COUNT(*) = 0, 'pass', 'flag')
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:65: FROM (SELECT utility_key, year FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY GROUP BY 1,2 HAVING COUNT(*) > 1)
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:66: UNION ALL
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:67: SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'T4', COUNT(*), '0', IFF(COUNT(*) = 0, 'pass', 'flag')
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:68: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:69: WHERE utility_key = '19876|VA' AND year = 2025 AND NOT (saidi_all = 306.7 AND saidi_ex_med = 144.4);
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:70: -- remaining tests follow the same pattern
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:71: ```
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:72: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:73: If this example task is fully implemented and enabled, the Data notes page could read `TEST_RESULTS` in place of the workbook's `Tests` sheet. The example below is not proof of a deployed scheduled task.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:74: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:75: ## Change control
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:76: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:77: - Each change to a SQL file is a commit whose message states the change and the test that verified it.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:78: - `CHANGELOG.md` records each load: row counts and any test whose result changed.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:79: - A definition change (a new event type in the storm index, a new peer) is recorded in `docs/04_decisions.md` with the alternative considered.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:80: - Report screenshots are not committed. The PDF export of each presented version is retained outside the repository with its date.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:81: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:82: ## Limitations
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:83: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:84: 1. Dominion's major-event rule differs from IEEE 1366. Peer comparisons are indicative.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:85: 2. EIA-861 identifies utilities, not customers. Attribution of load growth to data centres is an inference from the shape of the load.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:86: 3. The weather fit has ten observations. It is a check on one year, not a forecasting model.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:87: 4. EIA-930's DOM zone includes co-ops that take power through Dominion's transmission system. The zone load is slightly larger than Dominion's retail load.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:88: 5. The project does not measure the effect of the grid transformation programme. It reports what the public annual figure can and cannot show.
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:89: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:90: ## Artefacts not in the repository
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:91: 
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:92: | Artefact | Location | Reason |
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:93: |---|---|---|
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:94: | `Dominion_Reliability.pbix` (Import and DirectQuery copies) | Build machine, with a dated PDF export per presented version | Binary; embeds connection details |
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:95: | NOAA and EIA-930 raw downloads | `data/raw/` locally, ignored by git | 560 MB; recreated by the scripts |
docs/07_governance_and_runbook.md:96: | Snowflake account | `RELIABILITY` database on the project account | Credentials |
docs/08_transformations.md:1: # 08. Transformations
docs/08_transformations.md:2: 
docs/08_transformations.md:3: > **At a glance:** Quick read: worked examples show how source records become the reported three-year SAIDI, a storm county-day, annual demand and weather-model estimates.
docs/08_transformations.md:4: 
docs/08_transformations.md:5: The processing chain from source file to report figure, with worked examples on actual rows from the files in `data/` and the sources in `docs/01_sources.md`.
docs/08_transformations.md:6: 
docs/08_transformations.md:7: ## Processing chain
docs/08_transformations.md:8: 
docs/08_transformations.md:9: | Step | Layer | Operation | Code |
docs/08_transformations.md:10: |---|---|---|---|
docs/08_transformations.md:11: | 1 Load | RAW | The file is copied into a table with every column as text. No values are changed or dropped. | `01`, `05`, `06`: `COPY INTO` |
docs/08_transformations.md:12: | 2 Type | STG | Text is converted to numbers, dates and booleans. Thousands separators are removed. A value that fails conversion becomes NULL and T6 reports it. | `01`: `TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(x, ',', ''))` |
docs/08_transformations.md:13: | 3 Reshape | STG | EIA 2025: the two side-by-side column blocks become one column set with a `standard` label. NOAA: zone events are mapped to counties. EIA-930: rows are filtered to the DOM zone. | `01`: `UNION ALL`; `05`: zone join; `06`: `WHERE sub_region = 'DOM'` |
docs/08_transformations.md:14: | 4 De-duplicate | STG | One row per utility, state and year. Where a utility filed under both standards, the IEEE row is kept. | `01`: `QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() ... = 1` |
docs/08_transformations.md:15: | 5 Derive | MART | Customer-minutes and customer-interruptions are stored for aggregation. Peer groups are assigned by ID. | `02`: `FCT_RELIABILITY`, `DIM_UTILITY` |
docs/08_transformations.md:16: | 6 Index and fit | MART | Storm county-days per year. Weather fit. Hourly peaks, floor and top-50 ranking. | `05`, `06`, `07` |
docs/08_transformations.md:17: | 7 Test | MART | Twelve queries with stated expected results. | `04`, `05`, `06` |
docs/08_transformations.md:18: | 8 Present | Power BI | Measures sum numerators and divide once. | `powerbi/measures.dax` |
docs/08_transformations.md:19: 
docs/08_transformations.md:20: ## Worked example 1: three-year SAIDI, 133.0
docs/08_transformations.md:21: 
docs/08_transformations.md:22: **Step 1, the source row.** `data/input/Reliability_2025_States.csv`, utility 19876, Virginia. The file has three header rows and uses "." for blanks. Dominion files under the Other standard; the IEEE block is blank.
docs/08_transformations.md:23: 
docs/08_transformations.md:24: | Column in the file | Value as text |
docs/08_transformations.md:25: |---|---|
docs/08_transformations.md:26: | Utility Number | `19876` |
docs/08_transformations.md:27: | Utility Name | `Virginia Electric & Power Co` (the legal name filed with EIA) |
docs/08_transformations.md:28: | Ownership | `Investor Owned` |
docs/08_transformations.md:29: | IEEE SAIDI, all events | `.` (blank) |
docs/08_transformations.md:30: | Other SAIDI, all events | `306.700` |
docs/08_transformations.md:31: | Other SAIDI, without major event days | `144.400` |
docs/08_transformations.md:32: | Other SAIFI, without major event days | `1.320` |
docs/08_transformations.md:33: | Other CAIDI, without major event days | `109.394` |
docs/08_transformations.md:34: | Other customers | `2,675,735` |
docs/08_transformations.md:35: | Highest distribution voltage | `46.0` |
docs/08_transformations.md:36: | Outages recorded automatically | `Y` |
docs/08_transformations.md:37: 
docs/08_transformations.md:38: **Step 2, load and type.** `COPY INTO` with file format `FF_EIA_CSV` (`SKIP_HEADER = 3`, `NULL_IF = ('.')`) loads the row as text. The staging view converts `2,675,735` to 2675735 with `TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(oth_customers, ',', ''))` and `144.400` to `NUMBER(12,3)`.
docs/08_transformations.md:39: 
docs/08_transformations.md:40: **Step 3, reshape.** `STG.RELIABILITY_2025` is two `SELECT` statements joined by `UNION ALL`: one reads the IEEE block with `standard = 'IEEE 1366'`, the other reads the Other block with `standard = 'Other'`. Each keeps rows where that block contains a SAIDI. Dominion appears once, from the second `SELECT`.
docs/08_transformations.md:41: 
docs/08_transformations.md:42: **Step 4, union with history and de-duplicate.** PUDL supplies 2013 to 2024 in a long format with a row for each standard per utility, including empty rows. `STG.RELIABILITY_HIST` keeps rows with a value; for Dominion this is one row per year, all `Other`. `STG.RELIABILITY` unions history and 2025 and keeps one row per utility, state and year. Seven utilities filed under both standards in 2021 (including Pennyrile Rural Electric, Upper Cumberland EMC and several Tennessee municipals); the IEEE row is kept for each. Dominion has no duplicate.
docs/08_transformations.md:43: 
docs/08_transformations.md:44: **Step 5, derive.** `FCT_RELIABILITY` stores the products used for aggregation:
docs/08_transformations.md:45: 
docs/08_transformations.md:46: | Column | 2023 | 2024 | 2025 |
docs/08_transformations.md:47: |---|---|---|---|
docs/08_transformations.md:48: | `saidi_ex_med` | 123.322 | 131.300 | 144.400 |
docs/08_transformations.md:49: | `customers` | 2,612,104 | 2,635,267 | 2,675,735 |
docs/08_transformations.md:50: | `cust_minutes_ex_med` = saidi x customers | 322,129,889 | 346,010,557 | 386,376,134 |
docs/08_transformations.md:51: | `storm_saidi` = all - ex_med | 66.876 | 40.800 | 162.300 |
docs/08_transformations.md:52: 
docs/08_transformations.md:53: **Step 8, the measure.** `SAIDI 3yr ending 2025` is `AVERAGEX(VALUES(YEAR), [SAIDI ex MED])` filtered to 2023 to 2025, where `[SAIDI ex MED]` is `DIVIDE(SUM(CUST_MINUTES_EX_MED), SUM(CUSTOMERS))`. For a single utility this is (123.3 + 131.3 + 144.4) / 3 = **133.0**. Annual report, page 10: 133. The same measure for 2022 to 2024 is (136.0 + 123.3 + 131.3) / 3 = **130.2**. Annual report: 130.
docs/08_transformations.md:54: 
docs/08_transformations.md:55: **Verification.** Open the CSV and read 144.400 from the row for 19876 VA. Open `data/input/pudl_reliability_2013_2024.csv`, filter `utility_id_eia = 19876` and `state = VA` and read `saidi_wo_major_event_days_minutes` for 2023 and 2024. Average the three values. `python scripts/run_local_duckdb.py` performs the same steps and prints Q1.
docs/08_transformations.md:56: 
docs/08_transformations.md:57: ## Worked example 2: customer-weighted aggregation
docs/08_transformations.md:58: 
docs/08_transformations.md:59: The nine Virginia cooperatives that reported SAIFI ex-MED for 2025:
docs/08_transformations.md:60: 
docs/08_transformations.md:61: | Co-op | SAIFI ex-MED | Customers |
docs/08_transformations.md:62: |---|---|---|
docs/08_transformations.md:63: | A & N Electric | 2.279 | 37,914 |
docs/08_transformations.md:64: | Central Virginia Electric | 3.470 | 39,308 |
docs/08_transformations.md:65: | Mecklenburg Electric | 1.956 | 31,280 |
docs/08_transformations.md:66: | Northern Neck Electric | 2.168 | 21,079 |
docs/08_transformations.md:67: | Northern Virginia Electric (NOVEC) | 0.583 | 179,398 |
docs/08_transformations.md:68: | Powell Valley Electric | 4.267 | 8,406 |
docs/08_transformations.md:69: | Rappahannock Electric | 2.020 | 184,314 |
docs/08_transformations.md:70: | Shenandoah Valley Electric | 2.340 | 101,226 |
docs/08_transformations.md:71: | Southside Electric | 2.866 | 59,312 |
docs/08_transformations.md:72: 
docs/08_transformations.md:73: Mean of the nine ratios: **2.439**. Each utility carries equal weight regardless of size.
docs/08_transformations.md:74: 
docs/08_transformations.md:75: Customer-weighted: sum of (SAIFI x customers) = 1,249,316 interruptions, divided by 662,237 customers = **1.887**. All group figures in the report use the customer-weighted form, which is why `FCT_RELIABILITY` stores the products.
docs/08_transformations.md:76: 
docs/08_transformations.md:77: A tenth co-op, Prince George Electric, reported SAIDI but no SAIFI for 2025. A denominator summing all ten utilities' customers would include its 13,341 and give 1.849. `V_GROUP_INDEX` sums only the customers of utilities that reported the numerator, giving 1.887. The report's co-op index (each year relative to 2015) is 137.4 under either denominator because Prince George is absent in every year. Recorded as P18 in `docs/03_data_quality.md`.
docs/08_transformations.md:78: 
docs/08_transformations.md:79: ## Worked example 3: storm events to a county-day
docs/08_transformations.md:80: 
docs/08_transformations.md:81: **Source rows.** On 18 July 2025 NOAA recorded five Thunderstorm Wind events in Chesterfield County, all in episode 205761, `CZ_TYPE = C`, `CZ_FIPS = 41`:
docs/08_transformations.md:82: 
docs/08_transformations.md:83: | EVENT_ID | BEGIN_DATE_TIME | EVENT_TYPE | MAGNITUDE | BEGIN_LOCATION |
docs/08_transformations.md:84: |---|---|---|---|---|
docs/08_transformations.md:85: | 1284464 | 18-JUL-25 18:45 | Thunderstorm Wind | 50 kt | KINGSLAND |
docs/08_transformations.md:86: | 1284465 | 18-JUL-25 18:45 | Thunderstorm Wind | 50 kt | CHESTER |
docs/08_transformations.md:87: | 1284466 | 18-JUL-25 18:45 | Thunderstorm Wind | 50 kt | CHESTERFIELD |
docs/08_transformations.md:88: | 1284467 | 18-JUL-25 18:47 | Thunderstorm Wind | 50 kt | CHESTERFIELD |
docs/08_transformations.md:89: | 1284472 | 18-JUL-25 18:54 | Thunderstorm Wind | 50 kt | CHESTER |
docs/08_transformations.md:90: 
docs/08_transformations.md:91: **Step 3, county code.** For a county event the code is the state FIPS followed by the county FIPS padded to three digits: `'51' || LPAD('41', 3, '0')` = `51041`. `Thunderstorm Wind` maps to `line_risk = 'wind'`.
docs/08_transformations.md:92: 
docs/08_transformations.md:93: **Step 6, county-day.** `STORM_INDEX` groups by year, county and date. The five rows become one row: county 51041, 18 July 2025, wind = 1. Across the 104 Dominion counties in Virginia, 2025 has 796 county-days with a wind, winter or tropical event. 2024 had 465.
docs/08_transformations.md:94: 
docs/08_transformations.md:95: **Zone events.** The winter storm of 5 January 2025 was filed against forecast zones: `CZ_TYPE = Z`, `CZ_FIPS` 513 (Western Chesterfield), 514 (Eastern Chesterfield), 515 (Western Henrico), 516 (Eastern Henrico). The NWS zone-county correlation file maps each zone to its counties: zone 513 to Chesterfield (51041); zone 515 to Henrico (51087) and the City of Richmond (51760). Each mapped county receives a county-day. The correlation file has gaps (zone 514 maps only to Colonial Heights), which is one reason the index is treated as a measure of exposure rather than damage.
docs/08_transformations.md:96: 
docs/08_transformations.md:97: **Dominion's counties.** EIA's 2025 Service Territory file lists 104 counties and cities for utility 19876 in Virginia. EIA writes "Alexandria City" and NOAA writes "City of Alexandria", so both spellings are tried. T7 confirms all 104 resolve to a FIPS code.
docs/08_transformations.md:98: 
docs/08_transformations.md:99: ## Worked example 4: hourly demand to the top-50 count
docs/08_transformations.md:100: 
docs/08_transformations.md:101: **Source row.** `EIA930_SUBREGION_2025_Jan_Jun.csv`, line 171,407:
docs/08_transformations.md:102: 
docs/08_transformations.md:103: ```
docs/08_transformations.md:104: PJM,01/23/2025,8,DOM,24957,01/23/2025 8:00:00 AM,01/23/2025 1:00:00 PM
docs/08_transformations.md:105: ```
docs/08_transformations.md:106: 
docs/08_transformations.md:107: Balancing authority PJM, subregion DOM, hour ending 08:00 local on 23 January 2025, 24,957 MW. This is the highest hour in the record to the end of 2025.
docs/08_transformations.md:108: 
docs/08_transformations.md:109: **Step 3, filter and type.** `STG.EIA930_DOM_HOURLY` keeps rows where `balancing_authority = 'PJM' AND sub_region = 'DOM'` (72,864 of 6,077,232), parses both timestamps and keys on UTC so that clock-change days do not produce duplicates.
docs/08_transformations.md:110: 
docs/08_transformations.md:111: **Anomalous rows.** `EIA930_SUBREGION_2021_Jul_Dec.csv`, lines 216,193 and 216,213:
docs/08_transformations.md:112: 
docs/08_transformations.md:113: ```
docs/08_transformations.md:114: PJM,10/18/2021,23,DOM,1526640000,...
docs/08_transformations.md:115: PJM,10/18/2021,24,DOM,1038460000,...
docs/08_transformations.md:116: ```
docs/08_transformations.md:117: 
docs/08_transformations.md:118: The staging view retains the reported value in `demand_mw_reported` and sets `demand_mw` to NULL above 60,000 MW. T10 lists these two rows.
docs/08_transformations.md:119: 
docs/08_transformations.md:120: **Step 6, rank.** `DOM_TOP50_HOURS` ranks every hour by demand, once over the full record and once over hours to 31 December 2025. Top fifty by year at the second reference date:
docs/08_transformations.md:121: 
docs/08_transformations.md:122: | Year | Hours in the top 50 |
docs/08_transformations.md:123: |---|---|
docs/08_transformations.md:124: | 2024 | 3 |
docs/08_transformations.md:125: | 2025 | 47 |
docs/08_transformations.md:126: 
docs/08_transformations.md:127: **Annual floor and peak.** Lowest hour of 2019: 7,167 MW, 04:00 on 28 April. Lowest hour of 2025: 10,903 MW, 05:00 on 27 April. Change +52%. Highest hour of 2019: 19,803 MW, 08:00 on 31 January. Highest hour of 2025: 24,957 MW as above. Change +26%. `DOM_LOAD_PROFILE` makes the same comparison for each hour of the day: 04:00 +41%, 16:00 +27%.
docs/08_transformations.md:128: 
docs/08_transformations.md:129: ## Worked example 5: weather adjustment
docs/08_transformations.md:130: 
docs/08_transformations.md:131: Fit on 2015 to 2024: SAIDI ex-MED = 104.4 + 0.0514 x storm county-days. Mean SAIDI ex-MED over the ten years: 134.0.
docs/08_transformations.md:132: 
docs/08_transformations.md:133: | 2025 | Value |
docs/08_transformations.md:134: |---|---|
docs/08_transformations.md:135: | Storm county-days | 796 |
docs/08_transformations.md:136: | Predicted from weather | 104.4 + 0.0514 x 796 = **145.3** |
docs/08_transformations.md:137: | Actual | 144.4 |
docs/08_transformations.md:138: | Weather-adjusted | 144.4 - 145.3 + 134.0 = **133.1** |
docs/08_transformations.md:139: 
docs/08_transformations.md:140: For 2019 (851 county-days, actual 149.9) the same calculation gives 135.8.
docs/08_transformations.md:141: 
docs/08_transformations.md:142: ## Cleaning rules
docs/08_transformations.md:143: 
docs/08_transformations.md:144: | Rule | Applies to | Reason | Verification |
docs/08_transformations.md:145: |---|---|---|---|
docs/08_transformations.md:146: | Skip three header rows and treat "." as NULL | EIA-861 2025 | Format of the EIA export | Row count 977 |
docs/08_transformations.md:147: | Remove thousands separators before typing | Numeric columns from EIA | Values are exported as formatted text | T6 returns no rows |
docs/08_transformations.md:148: | All RAW columns are text | All sources | Typing failures are visible in a view rather than dropped at load | Row counts match the files |
docs/08_transformations.md:149: | Unpivot the IEEE and Other blocks | EIA-861 2025 | One column set with the standard as a label | Query B in `00`: 751 IEEE, 212 Other, 1 both, 13 neither |
docs/08_transformations.md:150: | Drop PUDL rows with no SAIDI under either standard | PUDL 2013 to 2024 | PUDL includes empty rows for both standards | Query D in `00` |
docs/08_transformations.md:151: | One row per utility, state and year, IEEE row kept | Union of history and 2025 | Customers counted once | T1 returns no rows |
docs/08_transformations.md:152: | Store customer-minutes and customer-interruptions | `FCT_RELIABILITY` | Customer-weighted aggregation | Query G in `00`: 2.439 vs 1.887 |
docs/08_transformations.md:153: | Group denominators include only utilities that reported the numerator | `V_GROUP_INDEX` | A utility with customers but no SAIFI must not dilute the group figure | P18 |
docs/08_transformations.md:154: | County code from FIPS, zones mapped through the NWS correlation file with a second pass on name | NOAA | Events must resolve to counties to match the service territory | T7 and T9 return no rows |
docs/08_transformations.md:155: | Count county-days, not events | `STORM_INDEX` | Multiple reports per storm | Grouping in `STORM_INDEX` |
docs/08_transformations.md:156: | Filter to PJM / DOM, key on UTC, cap at 60,000 MW | EIA-930 | Zone of interest, no clock-change duplicates, two anomalous rows | T10 returns 2, T11 and T12 return none |
docs/08_transformations.md:157: | Fit on 2015 to 2024, predict 2025 out of sample | `SAIDI_WEATHER_ADJUSTED` | The test year is excluded from the fit | Decision D6 |
docs/08_transformations.md:158: 
docs/08_transformations.md:159: ## Verifying a report figure
docs/08_transformations.md:160: 
docs/08_transformations.md:161: 1. Locate the figure in `docs/05_metrics_and_measures.md`, which names the table and the calculation.
docs/08_transformations.md:162: 2. Read the table definition in `docs/02_data_dictionary.md` to identify the source columns.
docs/08_transformations.md:163: 3. Open the source file in `data/input/`, or download it from the link in `docs/01_sources.md` and locate the rows.
docs/08_transformations.md:164: 4. Reproduce the calculation as above, or run `python scripts/run_local_duckdb.py` for the reliability mart.
docs/08_transformations.md:165: 5. If the figure does not match, the relevant test in `docs/03_data_quality.md` indicates where to look.
docs/README.md:1: # How to read the project
docs/README.md:2: 
docs/README.md:3: The [main README](../README.md) is the business summary. This folder holds the supporting evidence for anyone who wants to check the numbers or understand the choices.
docs/README.md:4: 
docs/README.md:5: **Start with three documents:**
docs/README.md:6: 
docs/README.md:7: 1. [Metrics and measures](05_metrics_and_measures.md): what SAIDI, SAIFI, CAIDI, weather exposure and demand numbers actually mean.
docs/README.md:8: 2. [Transformations](08_transformations.md): five worked examples tracing source data to calculations.
docs/README.md:9: 3. [Decisions](04_decisions.md): why the methods were chosen and where the findings are limited.
docs/README.md:10: 
docs/README.md:11: **For technical questions:** [Sources](01_sources.md) identifies datasets and attribution; [Data quality](03_data_quality.md) documents known anomalies and 12 checks; [Data dictionary](02_data_dictionary.md) lists the actual tables and fields; [Power BI guide](06_powerbi_report.md) maps findings to pages. [Conventions](00_conventions.md) and [Runbook](07_governance_and_runbook.md) are reference notes.
docs/README.md:12: 
docs/README.md:13: The `sql/` folder contains the executable calculations. These documents are explanatory records, not a substitute for the code or proof that any optional Snowflake automation has been deployed.
docs/README.md:14: 
docs/README.md:15: **Core conclusion:** The increase in reported outage minutes from 2023 to 2025 was more associated with interruption frequency than interruption duration. NOAA storm exposure was positively correlated with SAIDI in a small exploratory model; the public data does not establish the cause of outages or which feeder should be upgraded.

powerbi/Dominion_Reliability_PowerBI_Data.xlsx[README]:24: Method
powerbi/Dominion_Reliability_PowerBI_Data.xlsx[README]:25: SAIDI = SAIFI x CAIDI. Minutes and interruptions are summed per customer before dividing; ratios are never averaged across utilities. Multi-year figures are averages of annual values, as Dominion reports them.
powerbi/Dominion_Reliability_PowerBI_Data.xlsx[README]:26: Dominion files under EIA "Other standard" with its own major-storm rule, not IEEE 1366. Peer comparisons use multi-year averages and name the method.
powerbi/Dominion_Reliability_PowerBI_Data.xlsx[README]:27: Two counting changes: automatic outage recording from the 2015 filing; reported distribution boundary moves from 34.5 kV to 46 kV in 2020.

powerbi/Dominion_Reliability_PowerBI_Data.xlsx[Headline]:4: Row 2 is the only row Power BI imports (the table is A1:AN2). Row 3 explains each number. Every cell in row 2 is a formula pointing at the other sheets, so nothing here is typed in.
powerbi/measures.dax:1: // =====================================================================
powerbi/measures.dax:2: // Dominion reliability report: DAX measures and calculated columns, by table.
powerbi/measures.dax:3: // Columns keep Snowflake names; measures use metric names; plain-language
powerbi/measures.dax:4: // names are used in visual titles.
powerbi/measures.dax:5: // Aggregation: sum numerators, sum denominators, divide once.
powerbi/measures.dax:6: // =====================================================================
powerbi/measures.dax:7: 
powerbi/measures.dax:8: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
powerbi/measures.dax:9: // FCT_RELIABILITY: core reliability measures
powerbi/measures.dax:10: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
powerbi/measures.dax:11: 
powerbi/measures.dax:12: Total Customers = SUM(FCT_RELIABILITY[CUSTOMERS])
powerbi/measures.dax:13: 
powerbi/measures.dax:14: SAIDI ex MED = DIVIDE(SUM(FCT_RELIABILITY[CUST_MINUTES_EX_MED]), [Total Customers])
powerbi/measures.dax:15: 
powerbi/measures.dax:16: SAIFI ex MED = DIVIDE(SUM(FCT_RELIABILITY[CUST_INTERRUPTIONS_EX_MED]), [Total Customers])
powerbi/measures.dax:17: 
powerbi/measures.dax:18: CAIDI ex MED = DIVIDE([SAIDI ex MED], [SAIFI ex MED])
powerbi/measures.dax:19: 
powerbi/measures.dax:20: SAIDI all events = DIVIDE(SUM(FCT_RELIABILITY[CUST_MINUTES_ALL]), [Total Customers])
powerbi/measures.dax:21: 
powerbi/measures.dax:22: // EIA reporting difference: not independently established as NOAA storm damage.
powerbi/measures.dax:23: Major-event-day SAIDI = [SAIDI all events] - [SAIDI ex MED]
powerbi/measures.dax:24: Major-event-day share = DIVIDE([Major-event-day SAIDI], [SAIDI all events])
powerbi/measures.dax:25: 
powerbi/measures.dax:26: // Legacy names retained so existing Power BI visuals do not break.
powerbi/measures.dax:27: Storm SAIDI = [Major-event-day SAIDI]
powerbi/measures.dax:28: Storm share = [Major-event-day share]
powerbi/measures.dax:29: 
powerbi/measures.dax:30: // Three-year averages: the customer-weighted SAIDI for each year, then the mean of
powerbi/measures.dax:31: // the years. Matches Dominion's definition. Annual report: 133.0 and 130.2.
powerbi/measures.dax:32: SAIDI 3yr ending 2025 =
powerbi/measures.dax:33: CALCULATE(
powerbi/measures.dax:34:     AVERAGEX(VALUES(FCT_RELIABILITY[YEAR]), [SAIDI ex MED]),
powerbi/measures.dax:35:     FCT_RELIABILITY[YEAR] IN {2023, 2024, 2025}
powerbi/measures.dax:36: )
powerbi/measures.dax:37: 
powerbi/measures.dax:38: SAIDI 3yr ending 2024 =
powerbi/measures.dax:39: CALCULATE(

scripts/check_output_metrics.py:1: """Validate the small annual summaries committed with this project.
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:2: 
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:3: This checks arithmetic and that the saved CSVs agree with each other.
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:4: It does not rebuild the NOAA or EIA-930 raw-data pipelines.
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:5: """
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:6: import csv
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:7: from math import isclose, sqrt
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:8: from pathlib import Path
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:9: 
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:10: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:11: OUT = ROOT / "data" / "output"
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:12: 
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:13: 
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:14: def rows(filename):
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:15:     with (OUT / filename).open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:16:         records = list(csv.DictReader(stream))
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:17:     assert records, f"{filename} contains no rows"
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:18:     return records
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:19: 
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:20: 
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:21: def check(condition, message):
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:22:     if not condition:
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:23:         raise AssertionError(message)
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:24:     print("PASS:", message)
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:25: 
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:26: 
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:27: storm = {int(r["year"]): r for r in rows("storm_index.csv")}
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:28: paired = {int(r["year"]): r for r in rows("storm_vs_saidi.csv")}
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:29: check(set(storm) == set(paired) == set(range(2013, 2026)), "2013-2025 complete and matching")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:30: for year in storm:
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:31:     for col in ("storm_county_days", "wind_days", "winter_days", "tropical_days", "severe_gust_days"):
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:32:         check(storm[year][col] == paired[year][col], f"{year} {col} matches the annual index")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:33: check(int(storm[2024]["storm_county_days"]) == 465, "2024 storm county-days = 465")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:34: check(int(storm[2025]["storm_county_days"]) == 796, "2025 storm county-days = 796")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:35: 
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:36: training = [paired[y] for y in range(2015, 2025)]
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:37: x = [float(r["storm_county_days"]) for r in training]
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:38: y = [float(r["saidi_ex_med"]) for r in training]
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:39: mx, my = sum(x) / len(x), sum(y) / len(y)
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:40: ssx = sum((v - mx) ** 2 for v in x)
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:41: ssy = sum((v - my) ** 2 for v in y)
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:42: cross = sum((a - mx) * (b - my) for a, b in zip(x, y))
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:43: slope = cross / ssx
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:44: intercept = my - slope * mx
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:45: r = cross / sqrt(ssx * ssy)
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:46: pred_2025 = intercept + slope * float(paired[2025]["storm_county_days"])
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:47: actual_2025 = float(paired[2025]["saidi_ex_med"])
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:48: adjusted_2025 = actual_2025 - pred_2025 + my
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:49: check(0.69 < r < 0.71, f"2015-2024 Pearson correlation = {r:.3f}")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:50: check(0.050 < slope < 0.053, f"2015-2024 regression slope = {slope:.4f}")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:51: check(isclose(pred_2025, 145.3, abs_tol=0.15), f"2025 holdout prediction = {pred_2025:.2f} minutes")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:52: check(isclose(actual_2025, 144.4, abs_tol=0.001), "2025 actual SAIDI ex-MED = 144.4")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:53: check(isclose(adjusted_2025, 133.1, abs_tol=0.2), f"exploratory adjusted SAIDI = {adjusted_2025:.2f}")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:54: print("NOTE: regression checks confirm calculations, not weather causality.")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:55: 
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:56: load = {int(r["yr"]): r for r in rows("dom_annual.csv")}
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:57: check(2019 in load and 2025 in load, "DOM annual demand has 2019 and 2025")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:58: check(float(load[2019]["floor_mw"]) == 7167, "2019 minimum hourly demand = 7167 MW")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:59: check(float(load[2025]["floor_mw"]) == 10903, "2025 minimum hourly demand = 10903 MW")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:60: peak19 = max(float(load[2019]["winter_peak_mw"]), float(load[2019]["summer_peak_mw"]))
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:61: peak25 = max(float(load[2025]["winter_peak_mw"]), float(load[2025]["summer_peak_mw"]))
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:62: check(peak19 == 19803 and peak25 == 24957, "DOM annual peaks match source output")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:63: check(isclose((peak25 / peak19 - 1) * 100, 26, abs_tol=0.1), "peak-hour demand growth rounds to 26%")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:64: check(isclose((10903 / 7167 - 1) * 100, 52, abs_tol=0.2), "minimum-hour demand growth rounds to 52%")
scripts/check_output_metrics.py:65: print("All committed-output checks passed.")

scripts/download_noaa.sh:3: # File names carry a creation stamp (_cYYYYMMDD) that changes when NOAA re-issues a
scripts/download_noaa.sh:4: # year, so the script reads the directory listing to find the current names.
scripts/download_noaa.sh:5: set -euo pipefail
scripts/download_noaa.sh:6: BASE="https://www.ncei.noaa.gov/pub/data/swdi/stormevents/csvfiles"
scripts/download_noaa.sh:7: OUT="$(dirname "$0")/../data/raw/noaa"
scripts/download_noaa.sh:8: mkdir -p "$OUT" && cd "$OUT"
scripts/download_noaa.sh:9: curl -fsSL "$BASE/" \
scripts/download_noaa.sh:10:   | grep -o 'StormEvents_details-ftp_v1\.0_d20\(1[3-9]\|2[0-5]\)_c[0-9]\{8\}\.csv\.gz' \
scripts/download_noaa.sh:11:   | sort -u \
scripts/download_noaa.sh:12:   | while read -r f; do
scripts/download_noaa.sh:13:       echo "fetching $f"
scripts/download_noaa.sh:14:       curl -fsSLO "$BASE/$f"
scripts/download_noaa.sh:15:     done
scripts/download_noaa.sh:16: echo "---"
scripts/download_noaa.sh:17: ls -lh
scripts/download_noaa.sh:18: echo "expected: 13 files, 831,288 rows together after COPY INTO"

scripts/run_local_duckdb.py:25:     # TRY_TO_NUMBER(x, p, s) -> TRY_CAST(x AS DECIMAL(p,s)); TRY_TO_NUMBER(x) -> TRY_CAST(x AS BIGINT)

scripts/run_local_duckdb.py:27:     sql = re.sub(r"TRY_TO_NUMBER\(([^()]*(?:\([^()]*\))?[^()]*)\)", r"TRY_CAST(\1 AS BIGINT)", sql)
scripts/run_local_duckdb.py:28:     sql = re.sub(r"TRY_TO_DATE\(([^()]*)\)", r"TRY_CAST(\1 AS DATE)", sql)
scripts/run_local_duckdb.py:29:     sql = re.sub(r"TRY_TO_BOOLEAN\(([^()]*)\)", r"TRY_CAST(\1 AS BOOLEAN)", sql)
scripts/run_local_duckdb.py:30:     sql = re.sub(r"YEAR\(", "year(", sql)

sql/00_look_first.sql:61: 
sql/00_look_first.sql:62: -- G. After the staging views exist: mean of ratios vs customer-weighted ratio.
sql/00_look_first.sql:63: --    Expected 2.439 vs 1.887: ten co-ops filed and nine reported a SAIFI. The
sql/00_look_first.sql:64: --    weighted figure divides by the customers of those nine.
sql/00_look_first.sql:65: SELECT ROUND(AVG(saifi_ex_med), 3)                                                  AS avg_of_ratios,

sql/00_look_first.sql:67:              / SUM(CASE WHEN saifi_ex_med IS NOT NULL THEN customers END), 3)       AS customer_weighted,

sql/01_raw_staging.sql:8: -- ---------- 0. Setup (Snowflake only) ----------
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:9: CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS RELIABILITY;

sql/01_raw_staging.sql:14: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:15: -- One file format for EIA's Excel-exported CSVs and one for PUDL's clean CSVs
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:16: CREATE OR REPLACE FILE FORMAT RELIABILITY.RAW.FF_EIA_CSV
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:17:   TYPE = CSV
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:18:   SKIP_HEADER = 3                      -- EIA has three header rows
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:19:   FIELD_OPTIONALLY_ENCLOSED_BY = '"'   -- "2,675,735" comes quoted
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:20:   NULL_IF = ('.', '')                  -- EIA uses "." for blank
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:21:   EMPTY_FIELD_AS_NULL = TRUE;
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:22: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:23: CREATE OR REPLACE FILE FORMAT RELIABILITY.RAW.FF_PUDL_CSV
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:24:   TYPE = CSV
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:25:   SKIP_HEADER = 1
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:26:   FIELD_OPTIONALLY_ENCLOSED_BY = '"'
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:27:   NULL_IF = ('', 'NULL', '<NA>');
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:28: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:29: CREATE STAGE IF NOT EXISTS RELIABILITY.RAW.EIA_STAGE FILE_FORMAT = RELIABILITY.RAW.FF_EIA_CSV;
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:30: CREATE STAGE IF NOT EXISTS RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_STAGE FILE_FORMAT = RELIABILITY.RAW.FF_PUDL_CSV;
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:31: -- Upload via Snowsight (Data > Add Data > Load files into a stage) or SnowSQL:

sql/01_raw_staging.sql:33: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:34: -- ---------- 1. Raw tables: all columns text ----------

sql/01_raw_staging.sql:37: CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE RELIABILITY.RAW.EIA861_RELIABILITY_2025 (

sql/01_raw_staging.sql:51:

sql/01_raw_staging.sql:59:   short_form VARCHAR, standard VARCHAR, state VARCHAR, utility_id_eia VARCHAR,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:60:   utility_name_eia VARCHAR, data_maturity VARCHAR
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:61: );
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:62: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:63: CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_AMI (

sql/01_raw_staging.sql:74:   balancing_authority_code_eia VARCHAR, customer_class VARCHAR, business_model VARCHAR,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:75:   data_observed VARCHAR, entity_type VARCHAR, service_type VARCHAR, short_form VARCHAR,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:76:   utility_name_eia VARCHAR, customers VARCHAR, sales_mwh VARCHAR, sales_revenue VARCHAR,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:77:   data_maturity VARCHAR
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:78: );
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:79: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:80: -- Load the four files from the stages (upload them through Snowsight first:
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:81: -- Databases > RELIABILITY > RAW > Stages > EIA_STAGE / PUDL_STAGE > + Files)
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:82: COPY INTO RELIABILITY.RAW.EIA861_RELIABILITY_2025 FROM @RELIABILITY.RAW.EIA_STAGE/Reliability_2025_States.csv;
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:83: COPY INTO RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_RELIABILITY FROM @RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_STAGE/pudl_reliability_2013_2024.csv;
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:84: COPY INTO RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_AMI   FROM @RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_STAGE/pudl_ami.csv;

sql/01_raw_staging.sql:86: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:87: -- Check: expected 977, 24358, 127720, 198770
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:88: SELECT 'eia_2025' AS t, COUNT(*) AS n FROM RELIABILITY.RAW.EIA861_RELIABILITY_2025
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:89: UNION ALL SELECT 'pudl_rel',   COUNT(*) FROM RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_RELIABILITY
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:90: UNION ALL SELECT 'pudl_ami',   COUNT(*) FROM RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_AMI
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:91: UNION ALL SELECT 'pudl_sales', COUNT(*) FROM RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_SALES;
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:92: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:93: -- ---------- 2. Staging: one typed row per utility, state, year ----------
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:94: -- EIA 2025: unpivot the two column blocks. A utility that filed under IEEE
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:95: -- produces the IEEE row and one that filed under "Other" produces that row.
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:96: CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW RELIABILITY.STG.RELIABILITY_2025 AS
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:97: WITH t AS (
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:98:   SELECT
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:99:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(data_year)      AS year,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:100:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(utility_number) AS utility_id,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:101:     utility_name, state, ownership AS entity_type,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:102:     'IEEE 1366' AS standard,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:103:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(ieee_saidi_all,   ',',''), 12, 3) AS saidi_all,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:104:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(ieee_saifi_all,   ',',''), 12, 3) AS saifi_all,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:105:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(ieee_caidi_all,   ',',''), 12, 3) AS caidi_all,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:106:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(ieee_saidi_ex_med,',',''), 12, 3) AS saidi_ex_med,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:107:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(ieee_saifi_ex_med,',',''), 12, 3) AS saifi_ex_med,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:108:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(ieee_caidi_ex_med,',',''), 12, 3) AS caidi_ex_med,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:109:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(ieee_saidi_los,   ',',''), 12, 3) AS saidi_all_minus_los,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:110:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(ieee_customers,   ',',''))        AS customers,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:111:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(ieee_max_kv, 6, 1)                         AS max_kv,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:112:     ieee_auto_recorded = 'Y'                                 AS auto_recorded,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:113:     NULL::VARCHAR                                            AS momentary_def,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:114:     NULL::BOOLEAN                                            AS inactive_included
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:115:   FROM RELIABILITY.RAW.EIA861_RELIABILITY_2025
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:116:   WHERE ieee_saidi_all IS NOT NULL OR ieee_saidi_ex_med IS NOT NULL
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:117:   UNION ALL
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:118:   SELECT
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:119:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(data_year), TRY_TO_NUMBER(utility_number),
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:120:     utility_name, state, ownership,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:121:     'Other',
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:122:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(oth_saidi_all,   ',',''), 12, 3),
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:123:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(oth_saifi_all,   ',',''), 12, 3),
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:124:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(oth_caidi_all,   ',',''), 12, 3),
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:125:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(oth_saidi_ex_med,',',''), 12, 3),
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:126:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(oth_saifi_ex_med,',',''), 12, 3),
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:127:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(oth_caidi_ex_med,',',''), 12, 3),
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:128:     NULL,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:129:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(oth_customers,   ',','')),
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:130:     TRY_TO_NUMBER(oth_max_kv, 6, 1),
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:131:     oth_auto_recorded = 'Y',
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:132:     oth_momentary_def,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:133:     oth_inactive_included = 'Y'
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:134:   FROM RELIABILITY.RAW.EIA861_RELIABILITY_2025
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:135:   WHERE oth_saidi_all IS NOT NULL OR oth_saidi_ex_med IS NOT NULL
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:136: )
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:137: SELECT *, 'EIA-861 final 2025' AS source FROM t;
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:138: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:139: -- PUDL 2013-2024: one row per utility-state-year-standard. Both standards
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:140: -- appear as rows even when empty. Keep the rows with data.
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:141: CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW RELIABILITY.STG.RELIABILITY_HIST AS
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:142: SELECT
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:143:   YEAR(TRY_TO_DATE(report_date))                       AS year,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:144:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(utility_id_eia)                        AS utility_id,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:145:   utility_name_eia                                     AS utility_name,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:146:   state, entity_type,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:147:   CASE standard WHEN 'ieee_standard' THEN 'IEEE 1366' ELSE 'Other' END AS standard,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:148:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(saidi_w_med,  12, 3)                   AS saidi_all,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:149:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(saifi_w_med,  12, 3)                   AS saifi_all,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:150:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(caidi_w_med,  12, 3)                   AS caidi_all,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:151:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(saidi_wo_med, 12, 3)                   AS saidi_ex_med,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:152:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(saifi_wo_med, 12, 3)                   AS saifi_ex_med,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:153:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(caidi_wo_med, 12, 3)                   AS caidi_ex_med,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:154:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(saidi_w_med_minus_los, 12, 3)          AS saidi_all_minus_los,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:155:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(customers)                             AS customers,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:156:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(highest_distribution_voltage_kv, 6, 1) AS max_kv,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:157:   TRY_TO_BOOLEAN(outages_recorded_automatically)       AS auto_recorded,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:158:   momentary_interruption_definition                    AS momentary_def,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:159:   TRY_TO_BOOLEAN(inactive_accounts_included)           AS inactive_included,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:160:   'PUDL (EIA-861) 2013-2024'                            AS source
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:161: FROM RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_RELIABILITY
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:162: WHERE saidi_w_med IS NOT NULL OR saidi_wo_med IS NOT NULL;
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:163: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:164: -- Union, then de-duplicate: seven utilities filed under both standards in
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:165: -- 2021. The IEEE row is kept so customers are counted once.
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:166: CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW RELIABILITY.STG.RELIABILITY AS
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:167: SELECT *
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:168: FROM (
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:169:   SELECT * FROM RELIABILITY.STG.RELIABILITY_HIST
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:170:   UNION ALL
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:171:   SELECT * FROM RELIABILITY.STG.RELIABILITY_2025
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:172: )
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:173: QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:174:   PARTITION BY utility_id, state, year
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:175:   ORDER BY CASE standard WHEN 'IEEE 1366' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:176: ) = 1;
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:177: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:178: -- Smart meters: sum the customer classes, compute the AMI share
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:179: CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW RELIABILITY.STG.AMI AS
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:180: SELECT
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:181:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(utility_id_eia) AS utility_id, state,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:182:   YEAR(TRY_TO_DATE(report_date)) AS year,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:183:   SUM(TRY_TO_NUMBER(advanced_metering_infrastructure)) AS ami_meters,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:184:   SUM(TRY_TO_NUMBER(automated_meter_reading))          AS amr_meters,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:185:   SUM(TRY_TO_NUMBER(non_amr_ami))                      AS standard_meters,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:186:   ami_meters + COALESCE(amr_meters,0) + COALESCE(standard_meters,0) AS total_meters,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:187:   ami_meters / NULLIF(total_meters,0)                  AS ami_share
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:188: FROM RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_AMI
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:189: GROUP BY 1,2,3;
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:190: 
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:191: -- Sales: Dominion VA by class and service type
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:192: CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW RELIABILITY.STG.SALES AS
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:193: SELECT
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:194:   TRY_TO_NUMBER(utility_id_eia) AS utility_id, state,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:195:   YEAR(TRY_TO_DATE(report_date)) AS year,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:196:   customer_class, service_type,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:197:   SUM(TRY_TO_NUMBER(sales_mwh))     AS sales_mwh,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:198:   SUM(TRY_TO_NUMBER(customers))     AS customers,
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:199:   SUM(TRY_TO_NUMBER(sales_revenue)) AS revenue_usd
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:200: FROM RELIABILITY.RAW.PUDL_SALES
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:201: WHERE customer_class <> 'other'
sql/01_raw_staging.sql:202: GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5;
sql/02_mart.sql:1: -- =====================================================================
sql/02_mart.sql:2: -- DOMINION RELIABILITY MART  |  02: mart tables
sql/02_mart.sql:3: -- =====================================================================
sql/02_mart.sql:4: 
sql/02_mart.sql:5: -- Utility dimension with peer groups
sql/02_mart.sql:6: CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE RELIABILITY.MART.DIM_UTILITY COPY GRANTS AS
sql/02_mart.sql:7: SELECT DISTINCT
sql/02_mart.sql:8:   utility_id, state,
sql/02_mart.sql:9:   utility_id || '|' || state AS utility_key,
sql/02_mart.sql:10:   FIRST_VALUE(utility_name) OVER (PARTITION BY utility_id, state ORDER BY year DESC) AS utility_name,
sql/02_mart.sql:11:   FIRST_VALUE(entity_type)  OVER (PARTITION BY utility_id, state ORDER BY year DESC) AS entity_type,
sql/02_mart.sql:12:   CASE
sql/02_mart.sql:13:     WHEN utility_id = 19876 AND state = 'VA' THEN 'Dominion Energy Virginia'
sql/02_mart.sql:14:     WHEN state = 'VA' AND entity_type = 'Cooperative' THEN 'Virginia co-op'
sql/02_mart.sql:15:     WHEN utility_id = 733 AND state = 'VA' THEN 'Appalachian Power (VA)'
sql/02_mart.sql:16:     WHEN (utility_id, state) IN ((5416,'NC'),(3046,'NC'),(7140,'GA'),(195,'AL'),(17539,'SC')) THEN 'Southeast IOU peer'
sql/02_mart.sql:17:     WHEN (utility_id, state) IN ((1167,'MD'),(15270,'MD'),(14940,'PA'),(14715,'PA')) THEN 'Mid-Atlantic IOU peer'
sql/02_mart.sql:18:     WHEN (utility_id, state) IN ((6452,'FL'),(6455,'FL')) THEN 'Florida IOU peer'
sql/02_mart.sql:19:     ELSE 'Other'
sql/02_mart.sql:20:   END AS peer_group
sql/02_mart.sql:21: FROM RELIABILITY.STG.RELIABILITY;
sql/02_mart.sql:22: 
sql/02_mart.sql:23: -- Reliability fact. Stores the additive numerators used for aggregation:
sql/02_mart.sql:24: -- SAIDI = customer-minutes / customers.
sql/02_mart.sql:25: CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY COPY GRANTS AS
sql/02_mart.sql:26: SELECT
sql/02_mart.sql:27:   utility_id || '|' || state AS utility_key,
sql/02_mart.sql:28:   utility_id, state, year, standard, source,
sql/02_mart.sql:29:   customers,
sql/02_mart.sql:30:   saidi_all, saifi_all, caidi_all,
sql/02_mart.sql:31:   saidi_ex_med, saifi_ex_med, caidi_ex_med,
sql/02_mart.sql:32:   -- Legacy column names: these are *major-event-day exclusion differences*
sql/02_mart.sql:33:   -- in the EIA filing, not measured NOAA storm-caused outage minutes.
sql/02_mart.sql:34:   saidi_all - saidi_ex_med                       AS storm_saidi,
sql/02_mart.sql:35:   saifi_all - saifi_ex_med                       AS storm_saifi,
sql/02_mart.sql:36:   saidi_all * customers                          AS cust_minutes_all,
sql/02_mart.sql:37:   saidi_ex_med * customers                       AS cust_minutes_ex_med,
sql/02_mart.sql:38:   saifi_all * customers                          AS cust_interruptions_all,
sql/02_mart.sql:39:   saifi_ex_med * customers                       AS cust_interruptions_ex_med,
sql/02_mart.sql:40:   max_kv, auto_recorded, momentary_def, inactive_included
sql/02_mart.sql:41: FROM RELIABILITY.STG.RELIABILITY;
sql/02_mart.sql:42: 
sql/02_mart.sql:43: -- Year-on-year and rolling views for one utility-state
sql/02_mart.sql:44: CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW RELIABILITY.MART.V_TREND COPY GRANTS AS
sql/02_mart.sql:45: SELECT
sql/02_mart.sql:46:   f.utility_key, d.utility_name, d.peer_group, f.year, f.standard,
sql/02_mart.sql:47:   f.saidi_ex_med, f.saifi_ex_med, f.caidi_ex_med, f.saidi_all, f.saifi_all, f.storm_saidi, f.storm_saifi,
sql/02_mart.sql:48:   f.storm_saidi / NULLIF(f.saidi_all,0)                                           AS storm_share,
sql/02_mart.sql:49:   LAG(f.saidi_ex_med) OVER (PARTITION BY f.utility_key ORDER BY f.year)          AS saidi_ex_med_py,
sql/02_mart.sql:50:   LAG(f.saifi_ex_med) OVER (PARTITION BY f.utility_key ORDER BY f.year)          AS saifi_ex_med_py,
sql/02_mart.sql:51:   LAG(f.caidi_ex_med) OVER (PARTITION BY f.utility_key ORDER BY f.year)          AS caidi_ex_med_py,
sql/02_mart.sql:52:   f.saidi_ex_med / NULLIF(saidi_ex_med_py,0) - 1                                  AS saidi_yoy,
sql/02_mart.sql:53:   -- share of the SAIDI change that came from frequency: ln SAIDI = ln SAIFI + ln CAIDI.
sql/02_mart.sql:54:   -- NULLIF(x, 0) feeds LN a NULL instead of a zero: Snowflake raises on LN(0),
sql/02_mart.sql:55:   -- some utilities report 0, and LN(NULL) returns NULL.
sql/02_mart.sql:56:   -- When SAIDI changes by under about 2%, the denominator is near zero
sql/02_mart.sql:57:   -- and the attributed frequency share becomes misleadingly extreme.
sql/02_mart.sql:58:   CASE
sql/02_mart.sql:59:     WHEN ABS(LN(NULLIF(f.saidi_ex_med, 0) / NULLIF(saidi_ex_med_py, 0))) < 0.02
sql/02_mart.sql:60:       THEN NULL
sql/02_mart.sql:61:     ELSE LN(NULLIF(f.saifi_ex_med, 0) / NULLIF(saifi_ex_med_py, 0))
sql/02_mart.sql:62:       / NULLIF(LN(NULLIF(f.saidi_ex_med, 0) / NULLIF(saidi_ex_med_py, 0)), 0)
sql/02_mart.sql:63:   END AS frequency_share_of_change,
sql/02_mart.sql:64:   AVG(f.saidi_ex_med) OVER (PARTITION BY f.utility_key ORDER BY f.year
sql/02_mart.sql:65:                             ROWS BETWEEN 4 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW)             AS saidi_ex_med_5yr,
sql/02_mart.sql:66:   AVG(f.saifi_ex_med) OVER (PARTITION BY f.utility_key ORDER BY f.year
sql/02_mart.sql:67:                             ROWS BETWEEN 4 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW)             AS saifi_ex_med_5yr
sql/02_mart.sql:68: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY f
sql/02_mart.sql:69: JOIN RELIABILITY.MART.DIM_UTILITY d USING (utility_key);
sql/02_mart.sql:70: 
sql/02_mart.sql:71: -- Customer-weighted indices by peer group (used for the co-op benchmark).
sql/02_mart.sql:72: -- Each denominator counts only the customers of utilities that reported the
sql/02_mart.sql:73: -- numerator. A utility that filed SAIDI but not SAIFI (Prince George Electric,
sql/02_mart.sql:74: -- 2025) would otherwise dilute the group SAIFI. See docs/03, P18.
sql/02_mart.sql:75: CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW RELIABILITY.MART.V_GROUP_INDEX COPY GRANTS AS
sql/02_mart.sql:76: SELECT
sql/02_mart.sql:77:   d.peer_group, f.year,
sql/02_mart.sql:78:   COUNT(*)                                                         AS utilities,
sql/02_mart.sql:79:   SUM(f.customers)                                                 AS customers,
sql/02_mart.sql:80:   SUM(f.cust_interruptions_ex_med)
sql/02_mart.sql:81:     / NULLIF(SUM(CASE WHEN f.cust_interruptions_ex_med IS NOT NULL THEN f.customers END), 0) AS saifi_ex_med,
sql/02_mart.sql:82:   SUM(f.cust_minutes_ex_med)
sql/02_mart.sql:83:     / NULLIF(SUM(CASE WHEN f.cust_minutes_ex_med IS NOT NULL THEN f.customers END), 0)       AS saidi_ex_med,
sql/02_mart.sql:84:   SUM(f.cust_minutes_ex_med) / NULLIF(SUM(f.cust_interruptions_ex_med), 0)                    AS caidi_ex_med
sql/02_mart.sql:85: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY f
sql/02_mart.sql:86: JOIN RELIABILITY.MART.DIM_UTILITY d USING (utility_key)
sql/02_mart.sql:87: GROUP BY 1,2;
sql/03_analysis.sql:1: -- =====================================================================
sql/03_analysis.sql:2: -- DOMINION RELIABILITY MART  |  03: analysis queries
sql/03_analysis.sql:3: -- =====================================================================
sql/03_analysis.sql:4: 
sql/03_analysis.sql:5: -- Q1. Dominion's trend, decomposed into frequency and duration
sql/03_analysis.sql:6: -- Q1a: the trend with year-on-year decomposition
sql/03_analysis.sql:7: SELECT year, standard, saidi_ex_med, saifi_ex_med, caidi_ex_med,
sql/03_analysis.sql:8:        ROUND(saidi_yoy * 100, 1)                  AS saidi_yoy_pct,
sql/03_analysis.sql:9:        ROUND(frequency_share_of_change * 100, 0)   AS pct_of_change_from_frequency
sql/03_analysis.sql:10: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.V_TREND
sql/03_analysis.sql:11: WHERE utility_key = '19876|VA'
sql/03_analysis.sql:12: ORDER BY year;
sql/03_analysis.sql:13: 
sql/03_analysis.sql:14: -- Q1b: period decomposition (2019->2023 and 2023->2025)
sql/03_analysis.sql:15: WITH p AS (
sql/03_analysis.sql:16:   SELECT year, saidi_ex_med, saifi_ex_med, caidi_ex_med
sql/03_analysis.sql:17:   FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY WHERE utility_key = '19876|VA'
sql/03_analysis.sql:18: )
sql/03_analysis.sql:19: SELECT a.year AS from_year, b.year AS to_year,
sql/03_analysis.sql:20:        ROUND((b.saidi_ex_med / a.saidi_ex_med - 1) * 100, 1)                                   AS saidi_change_pct,
sql/03_analysis.sql:21:        ROUND(LN(b.saifi_ex_med / a.saifi_ex_med) / LN(b.saidi_ex_med / a.saidi_ex_med) * 100, 0) AS pct_from_frequency,
sql/03_analysis.sql:22:        ROUND(LN(b.caidi_ex_med / a.caidi_ex_med) / LN(b.saidi_ex_med / a.saidi_ex_med) * 100, 0) AS pct_from_duration
sql/03_analysis.sql:23: FROM p a JOIN p b ON (a.year, b.year) IN ((2019, 2023), (2023, 2025), (2015, 2025))
sql/03_analysis.sql:24: ORDER BY 1;
sql/03_analysis.sql:25: 
sql/03_analysis.sql:26: -- Q2. Storm share: major-event minutes vs ex-MED minutes
sql/03_analysis.sql:27: SELECT year, saidi_all, saidi_ex_med, storm_saidi,
sql/03_analysis.sql:28:        ROUND(storm_share * 100, 0)                                  AS storm_share_pct,
sql/03_analysis.sql:29:        ROUND(storm_saidi / NULLIF(saifi_all - saifi_ex_med, 0), 0)  AS storm_caidi_minutes
sql/03_analysis.sql:30: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.V_TREND
sql/03_analysis.sql:31: WHERE utility_key = '19876|VA'
sql/03_analysis.sql:32: ORDER BY year;
sql/03_analysis.sql:33: 
sql/03_analysis.sql:34: -- Q2b: correlation of storm minutes with ex-MED minutes
sql/03_analysis.sql:35: SELECT ROUND(CORR(storm_saidi, saidi_ex_med), 2) AS corr_storm_vs_daytoday
sql/03_analysis.sql:36: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY
sql/03_analysis.sql:37: WHERE utility_key = '19876|VA';
sql/03_analysis.sql:38: 
sql/03_analysis.sql:39: -- Q3. Dominion vs Virginia co-ops
sql/03_analysis.sql:40: -- Q3a: balanced panel of co-ops (>=10k customers, filed every year 2015-2025)
sql/03_analysis.sql:41: CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW RELIABILITY.MART.V_VA_COOP_PANEL AS
sql/03_analysis.sql:42: SELECT f.utility_key
sql/03_analysis.sql:43: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY f
sql/03_analysis.sql:44: JOIN RELIABILITY.MART.DIM_UTILITY d USING (utility_key)
sql/03_analysis.sql:45: WHERE d.peer_group = 'Virginia co-op' AND f.year BETWEEN 2015 AND 2025
sql/03_analysis.sql:46: GROUP BY f.utility_key
sql/03_analysis.sql:47: HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT f.year) = 11 AND MAX(f.customers) >= 10000;
sql/03_analysis.sql:48: 
sql/03_analysis.sql:49: -- Q3b: Dominion vs the co-op index, year by year
sql/03_analysis.sql:50: WITH coop AS (
sql/03_analysis.sql:51:   SELECT year,
sql/03_analysis.sql:52:          SUM(cust_interruptions_ex_med) / SUM(customers) AS coop_saifi,
sql/03_analysis.sql:53:          SUM(cust_minutes_ex_med)       / SUM(customers) AS coop_saidi,
sql/03_analysis.sql:54:          COUNT(*) AS n_coops
sql/03_analysis.sql:55:   FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY
sql/03_analysis.sql:56:   WHERE utility_key IN (SELECT utility_key FROM RELIABILITY.MART.V_VA_COOP_PANEL)
sql/03_analysis.sql:57:   GROUP BY year
sql/03_analysis.sql:58: ), dom AS (
sql/03_analysis.sql:59:   SELECT year, saifi_ex_med AS dom_saifi, saidi_ex_med AS dom_saidi
sql/03_analysis.sql:60:   FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY WHERE utility_key = '19876|VA'
sql/03_analysis.sql:61: )
sql/03_analysis.sql:62: SELECT d.year, n_coops,
sql/03_analysis.sql:63:        ROUND(dom_saifi, 3) AS dom_saifi, ROUND(coop_saifi, 3) AS coop_saifi,
sql/03_analysis.sql:64:        ROUND(dom_saifi / coop_saifi, 3) AS dom_relative_to_coops,
sql/03_analysis.sql:65:        ROUND(dom_saifi / LAG(dom_saifi)  OVER (ORDER BY d.year) - 1, 3) AS dom_yoy,
sql/03_analysis.sql:66:        ROUND(coop_saifi / LAG(coop_saifi) OVER (ORDER BY d.year) - 1, 3) AS coop_yoy
sql/03_analysis.sql:67: FROM dom d JOIN coop c USING (year)
sql/03_analysis.sql:68: WHERE d.year >= 2015
sql/03_analysis.sql:69: ORDER BY year;
sql/03_analysis.sql:70: 
sql/03_analysis.sql:71: -- Q3c: correlation of yearly changes, and the change in Dominion's position
sql/03_analysis.sql:72: -- relative to the co-ops (3-year averages)
sql/03_analysis.sql:73: WITH coop AS (
sql/03_analysis.sql:74:   SELECT year, SUM(cust_interruptions_ex_med) / SUM(customers) AS coop_saifi
sql/03_analysis.sql:75:   FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY
sql/03_analysis.sql:76:   WHERE utility_key IN (SELECT utility_key FROM RELIABILITY.MART.V_VA_COOP_PANEL)
sql/03_analysis.sql:77:   GROUP BY year
sql/03_analysis.sql:78: ), j AS (
sql/03_analysis.sql:79:   SELECT f.year, f.saifi_ex_med AS dom_saifi, c.coop_saifi,
sql/03_analysis.sql:80:          LN(f.saifi_ex_med / LAG(f.saifi_ex_med) OVER (ORDER BY f.year)) AS dom_dlog,
sql/03_analysis.sql:81:          LN(c.coop_saifi    / LAG(c.coop_saifi)  OVER (ORDER BY f.year)) AS coop_dlog
sql/03_analysis.sql:82:   FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY f JOIN coop c USING (year)
sql/03_analysis.sql:83:   WHERE f.utility_key = '19876|VA' AND f.year >= 2015
sql/03_analysis.sql:84: )
sql/03_analysis.sql:85: SELECT
sql/03_analysis.sql:86:   ROUND(CORR(dom_dlog, coop_dlog), 2)                                                        AS corr_of_yoy_changes,
sql/03_analysis.sql:87:   ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN year BETWEEN 2015 AND 2017 THEN dom_saifi / coop_saifi END), 3)         AS relative_2015_17,
sql/03_analysis.sql:88:   ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN year BETWEEN 2023 AND 2025 THEN dom_saifi / coop_saifi END), 3)         AS relative_2023_25,
sql/03_analysis.sql:89:   ROUND((relative_2023_25 / relative_2015_17 - 1) * 100, 1)                                   AS dominion_relative_improvement_pct
sql/03_analysis.sql:90: FROM j;
sql/03_analysis.sql:91: 
sql/03_analysis.sql:92: -- Q3d: leave-one-out robustness check
sql/03_analysis.sql:93: WITH panel AS (SELECT utility_key FROM RELIABILITY.MART.V_VA_COOP_PANEL),
sql/03_analysis.sql:94: dom AS (SELECT year, saifi_ex_med AS dom_saifi FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY WHERE utility_key = '19876|VA'),
sql/03_analysis.sql:95: loo AS (
sql/03_analysis.sql:96:   SELECT p.utility_key AS dropped, f.year,
sql/03_analysis.sql:97:          SUM(f.cust_interruptions_ex_med) / SUM(f.customers) AS coop_saifi
sql/03_analysis.sql:98:   FROM panel p
sql/03_analysis.sql:99:   JOIN RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY f
sql/03_analysis.sql:100:     ON f.utility_key IN (SELECT utility_key FROM panel) AND f.utility_key <> p.utility_key
sql/03_analysis.sql:101:   GROUP BY p.utility_key, f.year
sql/03_analysis.sql:102: )
sql/03_analysis.sql:103: SELECT d2.utility_name AS dropped_coop,
sql/03_analysis.sql:104:        ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN l.year BETWEEN 2015 AND 2017 THEN dom_saifi / coop_saifi END), 3) AS rel_2015_17,
sql/03_analysis.sql:105:        ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN l.year BETWEEN 2023 AND 2025 THEN dom_saifi / coop_saifi END), 3) AS rel_2023_25,
sql/03_analysis.sql:106:        ROUND((rel_2023_25 / rel_2015_17 - 1) * 100, 1) AS dominion_relative_improvement_pct
sql/03_analysis.sql:107: FROM loo l JOIN dom USING (year) JOIN RELIABILITY.MART.DIM_UTILITY d2 ON d2.utility_key = l.dropped
sql/03_analysis.sql:108: GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 4;
sql/03_analysis.sql:109: 
sql/03_analysis.sql:110: -- Q4. Effect of smart meter rollout on measured frequency
sql/03_analysis.sql:111: -- Event study across all IOUs: SAIFI (net of the national year median) before
sql/03_analysis.sql:112: -- and after the year AMI share first passed 50%, for utilities below 20% two years earlier.
sql/03_analysis.sql:113: WITH base AS (
sql/03_analysis.sql:114:   SELECT f.utility_key, f.year, f.standard, a.ami_share,
sql/03_analysis.sql:115:          LN(f.saifi_ex_med) - MEDIAN(LN(f.saifi_ex_med)) OVER (PARTITION BY f.year) AS lsaifi_adj
sql/03_analysis.sql:116:   FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY f
sql/03_analysis.sql:117:   JOIN RELIABILITY.MART.DIM_UTILITY d USING (utility_key)
sql/03_analysis.sql:118:   LEFT JOIN RELIABILITY.STG.AMI a ON a.utility_id = f.utility_id AND a.state = f.state AND a.year = f.year
sql/03_analysis.sql:119:   WHERE d.entity_type = 'Investor Owned' AND f.customers >= 100000 AND f.saifi_ex_med > 0
sql/03_analysis.sql:120: ),
sql/03_analysis.sql:121: one_std AS (SELECT utility_key FROM base GROUP BY 1 HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT standard) = 1),
sql/03_analysis.sql:122: rollout AS (
sql/03_analysis.sql:123:   SELECT utility_key, MIN(year) AS y0 FROM base WHERE ami_share >= 0.5 GROUP BY 1
sql/03_analysis.sql:124: ),
sql/03_analysis.sql:125: eligible AS (
sql/03_analysis.sql:126:   SELECT r.utility_key, r.y0
sql/03_analysis.sql:127:   FROM rollout r
sql/03_analysis.sql:128:   JOIN base b ON b.utility_key = r.utility_key AND b.year = r.y0 - 2
sql/03_analysis.sql:129:   WHERE b.ami_share < 0.2 AND r.utility_key IN (SELECT utility_key FROM one_std)
sql/03_analysis.sql:130: ),
sql/03_analysis.sql:131: rel AS (
sql/03_analysis.sql:132:   SELECT b.utility_key, b.year - e.y0 AS years_from_rollout,
sql/03_analysis.sql:133:          b.lsaifi_adj - b0.lsaifi_adj AS change_vs_two_years_before
sql/03_analysis.sql:134:   FROM eligible e
sql/03_analysis.sql:135:   JOIN base b  ON b.utility_key = e.utility_key
sql/03_analysis.sql:136:   JOIN base b0 ON b0.utility_key = e.utility_key AND b0.year = e.y0 - 2
sql/03_analysis.sql:137:   WHERE b.year BETWEEN e.y0 - 3 AND e.y0 + 3
sql/03_analysis.sql:138: )
sql/03_analysis.sql:139: SELECT years_from_rollout, COUNT(*) AS utilities,
sql/03_analysis.sql:140:        ROUND(AVG(change_vs_two_years_before) * 100, 1)    AS avg_change_pct,
sql/03_analysis.sql:141:        ROUND(MEDIAN(change_vs_two_years_before) * 100, 1) AS median_change_pct
sql/03_analysis.sql:142: FROM rel GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1;
sql/03_analysis.sql:143: 
sql/03_analysis.sql:144: -- Q5. Dominion VA sales and customers by class
sql/03_analysis.sql:145: SELECT year,
sql/03_analysis.sql:146:        SUM(CASE WHEN customer_class = 'commercial'  THEN sales_mwh END) / 1e6 AS commercial_twh,
sql/03_analysis.sql:147:        SUM(CASE WHEN customer_class = 'residential' THEN sales_mwh END) / 1e6 AS residential_twh,
sql/03_analysis.sql:148:        SUM(CASE WHEN customer_class = 'commercial'  AND service_type = 'bundled' THEN customers END) AS commercial_customers
sql/03_analysis.sql:149: FROM RELIABILITY.STG.SALES
sql/03_analysis.sql:150: WHERE utility_id = 19876 AND state = 'VA' AND year >= 2013
sql/03_analysis.sql:151: GROUP BY year ORDER BY year;
sql/04_tests.sql:1: -- =====================================================================
sql/04_tests.sql:2: -- DOMINION RELIABILITY MART  |  04: data quality tests
sql/04_tests.sql:3: -- Each query should return zero rows (or the expected count). Run after
sql/04_tests.sql:4: -- every load. In Snowflake, wrap them in a TASK scheduled for next October
sql/04_tests.sql:5: -- when EIA publishes the next year.
sql/04_tests.sql:6: -- =====================================================================
sql/04_tests.sql:7: 
sql/04_tests.sql:8: -- T1. No duplicate utility-state-year after de-duplication
sql/04_tests.sql:9: SELECT utility_key, year, COUNT(*) AS n
sql/04_tests.sql:10: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY
sql/04_tests.sql:11: GROUP BY 1,2 HAVING COUNT(*) > 1;
sql/04_tests.sql:12: 
sql/04_tests.sql:13: -- T2. SAIDI = SAIFI x CAIDI within rounding
sql/04_tests.sql:14: SELECT utility_key, year, saidi_ex_med, saifi_ex_med * caidi_ex_med AS recomputed
sql/04_tests.sql:15: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY
sql/04_tests.sql:16: WHERE saifi_ex_med > 0 AND caidi_ex_med > 0
sql/04_tests.sql:17:   AND ABS(saidi_ex_med - saifi_ex_med * caidi_ex_med) / saidi_ex_med > 0.02;
sql/04_tests.sql:18: 
sql/04_tests.sql:19: -- T3. All-events SAIDI is not lower than ex-MED SAIDI
sql/04_tests.sql:20: SELECT utility_key, year, saidi_all, saidi_ex_med
sql/04_tests.sql:21: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY
sql/04_tests.sql:22: WHERE saidi_all < saidi_ex_med - 0.01;
sql/04_tests.sql:23: 
sql/04_tests.sql:24: -- T4. Dominion VA 2025 exists exactly once and matches EIA's published values.
sql/04_tests.sql:25: -- A LEFT JOIN also flags a missing row; the former WHERE clause did not.
sql/04_tests.sql:26: WITH expected AS (
sql/04_tests.sql:27:   SELECT '19876|VA' AS utility_key, 2025 AS year
sql/04_tests.sql:28: )
sql/04_tests.sql:29: SELECT e.utility_key, e.year, f.saidi_all, f.saidi_ex_med, f.customers
sql/04_tests.sql:30: FROM expected e
sql/04_tests.sql:31: LEFT JOIN RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY f
sql/04_tests.sql:32:   ON f.utility_key = e.utility_key AND f.year = e.year
sql/04_tests.sql:33: WHERE f.utility_key IS NULL
sql/04_tests.sql:34:    OR f.saidi_all IS DISTINCT FROM 306.7
sql/04_tests.sql:35:    OR f.saidi_ex_med IS DISTINCT FROM 144.4
sql/04_tests.sql:36:    OR f.customers IS DISTINCT FROM 2675735;
sql/04_tests.sql:37: 
sql/04_tests.sql:38: -- T5. Row counts and customer totals by year
sql/04_tests.sql:39: SELECT year, COUNT(*) AS utilities, SUM(customers) AS customers
sql/04_tests.sql:40: FROM RELIABILITY.MART.FCT_RELIABILITY GROUP BY year ORDER BY year;
sql/04_tests.sql:41: 
sql/04_tests.sql:42: -- T6. Raw numeric values that failed TRY_TO_NUMBER after separator removal
sql/04_tests.sql:43: SELECT utility_number, utility_name, state, oth_customers
sql/04_tests.sql:44: FROM RELIABILITY.RAW.EIA861_RELIABILITY_2025
sql/04_tests.sql:45: WHERE oth_customers IS NOT NULL AND TRY_TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(oth_customers, ',', '')) IS NULL;
